	public void ApplyHeadOffsetFromMount(ref Vector2 pos)
	{
		if (!mount.Active)
		{
			return;
		}
		int frame = mount.Frame;
		Vector2 vector = Vector2.Zero;
		if (mount.Type == 61)
		{
			vector = new Vector2(-2f, 20f) * Directions;
		}
		else if (mount.Type == 56)
		{
			vector = new Vector2(6f, 18f) * Directions;
			switch (frame)
			{
			case 1:
			case 2:
				vector += new Vector2(0f, 2f) * Directions;
				break;
			case 6:
			case 7:
			case 8:
			case 9:
				vector += new Vector2(0f, 2f) * Directions;
				break;
			}
		}
		else if (mount.Type == 55)
		{
			vector = new Vector2(0f, 18f) * Directions;
			switch (frame)
			{
			case 1:
				vector += new Vector2(-2f, 0f) * Directions;
				break;
			case 2:
			case 3:
				vector += new Vector2(-4f, -2f) * Directions;
				break;
			case 4:
			case 5:
			case 6:
			case 7:
			case 8:
			case 9:
			case 10:
				vector += new Vector2(4f, 2f) * Directions;
				break;
			case 11:
				vector += new Vector2(4f, 2f) * Directions;
				break;
			case 12:
			case 13:
				vector += new Vector2(6f, 0f) * Directions;
				break;
			case 14:
				vector += new Vector2(6f, 2f) * Directions;
				break;
			case 15:
				vector += new Vector2(4f, 2f) * Directions;
				break;
			}
		}
		else if (mount.Type == 54)
		{
			vector = new Vector2(23f, -8f) * Directions;
			switch (frame)
			{
			case 5:
				vector += new Vector2(0f, 4f) * Directions;
				break;
			case 6:
				vector += new Vector2(2f, 2f) * Directions;
				break;
			case 7:
				vector += new Vector2(2f, 0f) * Directions;
				break;
			case 8:
			case 9:
			case 11:
			case 12:
			case 13:
			case 15:
				vector += new Vector2(4f, 4f) * Directions;
				break;
			case 10:
			case 14:
				vector += new Vector2(4f, 2f) * Directions;
				break;
			case 16:
			case 17:
			case 18:
			case 19:
			case 20:
				vector += new Vector2(4f, 4f) * Directions;
				break;
			}
		}
		pos += vector;
	}

	public void FlipItemLocationAndRotationForGravity()

	public static Vector2 DrawPlayer_GetMountOffsetForFaceAcc(ref PlayerDrawSet drawinfo, ref bool isVelociraptor, ref bool hasRockGolemHead)
	{
		Vector2 pos = Vector2.Zero;
		isVelociraptor = false;
		hasRockGolemHead = drawinfo.drawPlayer.head == 241;
		if (drawinfo.drawPlayer.mount.Active)
		{
			switch (drawinfo.drawPlayer.mount.Type)
			{
			case 52:
				pos = new Vector2(28f, -2f) * drawinfo.drawPlayer.Directions;
				break;
			case 54:
				drawinfo.drawPlayer.ApplyHeadOffsetFromMount(ref pos);
				pos -= drawinfo.drawPlayer.GetHelmetOffsetAddonFromMount();
				isVelociraptor = true;
				break;
			case 55:
			case 56:
			case 61:
				drawinfo.drawPlayer.ApplyHeadOffsetFromMount(ref pos);
				pos -= drawinfo.drawPlayer.GetHelmetOffsetAddonFromMount();
				break;
			}
		}
		return pos;
	}

  private mountHeldAnchor(
    mtype: number, useStyle: number | null, holdStyle: number, animating: boolean,
    isFishingPole = false,
  ): { dx: number; dy: number; resetX?: number; resetY?: number } | null {
    const d = 1;   // dir=1 空间（offX 语义同域：直接 += / 赋值）
    if (mtype === 52) {
      // AdjustmentsForWolfMount（:1785-1840）；钓竿 flag4（fishingPole!=0）豁免原锚
      if (isFishingPole) return null;
      if (useStyle === 2) return { dx: d * 14, dy: -4 };
      if (useStyle === 9) return animating ? { dx: d * 18, dy: -4 } : { dx: d * 14, dy: -18 };
      if (useStyle === 8) return { dx: 0, dy: 0, resetX: 14 + d * 12, resetY: 12 + 2 };
      if (useStyle === 14) return { dx: d * 5, dy: -2 };
      if (useStyle === 5) return { dx: d * 4, dy: -4 };
      if (holdStyle === 5) return { dx: d * 17, dy: -8 };
      if (holdStyle === 1 && !animating) return { dx: d * 14, dy: -6 };
      if (holdStyle === 2 && !animating) return { dx: d * 17, dy: 4 };
      return { dx: 0, dy: 0, resetX: 14, resetY: 12 };   // else：ItemLocation = vector2
    }
    if (mtype === 54) {
      // ApplyItemPositionOffsetFromMount（:50671-50770）：仅 animating || holdStyle>0
      if (!animating && holdStyle <= 0) return null;
      if (isFishingPole) return { dx: -2, dy: 0 };
      if (useStyle === 2) return animating ? { dx: 7, dy: -4 } : { dx: 3, dy: 2 };
      if (useStyle === 9) return animating ? { dx: 14, dy: -10 } : { dx: 3, dy: 2 };
      if (useStyle === 11) return animating ? { dx: 4, dy: 0 } : { dx: 1, dy: 0 };
      if (useStyle === 8) return { dx: 6, dy: 0 };
      if (useStyle === 14) return animating ? { dx: -6, dy: 6 } : { dx: -10, dy: 10 };
      if (holdStyle === 5) return { dx: 6, dy: 0 };
      if (holdStyle === 1 && !animating) return { dx: 1, dy: 4 };
      if (holdStyle === 2) return { dx: 6, dy: 0 };
      return null;
    }
    return null;
  }

  private drawUseItem(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, p: Player, swing: { t: number; dur: number; item: number; useStyle?: number; aim?: number; noGraphic?: boolean }, flicker: FlickerClock | null = null) {
    const ar = this.atlasIcon(swing.item);
    const icon = ar ? null : this.itemIcon(swing.item);
    const pAnim = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, swing.t / swing.dur));
    const fw = ar ? ar.sw : 14, fh = ar ? ar.sh : 14;
    const us = swing.useStyle ?? 1;
    // ★aim 方向化（Player.cs:46730/46737：itemRotation = Atan2(aimY*dir, aimX*dir)——
    //  原版朝左时以翻转 sprite + 该角绘制；本项目是镜像空间，等价本地绘制角 = π-aim
    //  （镜像共轭 mirror∘rot(θ)=rot(−θ)∘mirror，模 2π 后世界指向与 aim 一致）。
    //  useStyle 5（法杖/枪弓）与 13（短剑）消费；aim 未传取 0（正前方）
    const aim = p.facing === -1 ? Math.PI - (swing.aim ?? 0) : (swing.aim ?? 0);
    // → { rot, offX, offY, drawOX, drawOY }（dir=1 空间）；hide = 原版把 itemLocation
    // 挪到 -1000（不画）。drawO* = itemLocation（握点）在精灵矩形内的锚定偏移，
    // 默认底左角 (0,-fh)（PlayerDrawLayers.cs:3260 origin=(W/2-W/2*dir, H)）
    let rot = 0, offX = 0, offY = 0, hide = false, drawOX = 0, drawOY = -fh;
    // useStyle 1 三段持位 tier（Player.cs:49957-50095）——case 1 与 default 共用
    const swingTier = (ph: 0 | 1 | 2): { x: number; y: number } => {
      const xT = (w: number) => ph === 0
        ? w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w > 32 ? 14 : 10
        : ph === 1
          ? w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w > 32 ? 18 : 10
          : w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w >= 48 ? 18 : w > 32 ? 14 : 6;
      const yT = (h: number) => ph === 0 ? 24
        : h > 64 ? 14 : h > 52 ? 12 : ph === 1 && h > 32 ? 8 : 10;
      // phase2（起手）持位在身后（:50071 `center - (w/2-num6)*dir`）
      return ph === 2
        ? { x: -(fw * 0.5 - xT(fw)), y: yT(fh) }
        : { x: fw * 0.5 - xT(fw), y: yT(fh) };
    };
    switch (us) {
      case 1: {
        // useStyle=1（:49939-50108）1:1：约 200° 线性挥砍弧 + 三段持位。
        // ★时段方向：pAnim ≡ itemAnimation/itemAnimationMax（随 t 递减，与 rot 公式同源）——
        //  末段(anim<0.333max ⟺ pAnim≤1/3)→前持位(phase0) / 中段→(phase1) /
        //  起手(anim≥0.666max ⟺ pAnim≥2/3)→后举位(phase2)。此前按"递增进度"整段接反
        rot = (pAnim - 0.5) * -3.5 - 0.3;
        const seg = pAnim <= 1 / 3 ? swingTier(0) : pAnim < 2 / 3 ? swingTier(1) : swingTier(2);
        offX = seg.x; offY = seg.y;
        break;
      }
      case 2: {
        // 咀嚼（:50183-50224，DefaultToFood 非 gulp 族：蘑菇 5/蜂蜜鳍 2314）：
        // 手臂伸缩档 num15=8/6/4/2 → offX = num15+10-fw/2，Y = 中心 + fh/2-4；rot 不变
        const n14 = 1 - pAnim;
        const n15 = n14 <= 0.25 ? 8 : n14 <= 0.5 ? 6 : n14 <= 0.75 ? 4 : 2;
        offX = n15 + 10 - fw * 0.5;
        offY = p.h * 0.5 + fh * 0.5 - 4;
        break;
      }
      case 3: {
        // 雨伞（:50279-50312）：起手段（anim>0.666max ⟺ pAnim>0.666）itemLocation=-1000 不画
        if (pAnim > 0.666) { hide = true; break; }
        rot = 0.8;
        offX = fw * 0.5 - 4 - Math.max(pAnim * fw * 1.2 - 10, 8);
        offY = 24;
        // 946/4707 特判（Player.cs:50302-50305 itemLocation.X-=6*dir +
        // PlayerDrawLayers.cs:3314-3318 绘制位 Y-=22*gravDir、绘制角=-1.57*-direction）
        const vid3 = viIdFromKey(ITEM_DEFS[swing.item]?.key ?? '');
        if (vid3 === 946 || vid3 === 4707) { offX -= 6; offY -= 22; rot = 1.57; }
        break;
      }
      case 4: {
        // 举过头（:50313-50338）：rot=0；offX = fw/2-13（3601/5114/5120 再 -10）
        // offY = fh/2+4（5114 再 -2）——使用中身体行 2（双手举过头顶）
        const vid4 = viIdFromKey(ITEM_DEFS[swing.item]?.key ?? '');
        offX = fw * 0.5 - 13 - (vid4 === 3601 || vid4 === 5114 || vid4 === 5120 ? 10 : 0);
        offY = fh * 0.5 + 4 + (vid4 === 5114 ? -2 : 0);
        break;
      }
      case 5: {
        // 朝鼠标静持（:50339-50422 + :46708 itemRotation=瞄准角）：
        // 法杖族钉 MountedCenter+瞄准向量×6；默认 X=中心-2*dir、Y=中心-fh/2
        const vid = viIdFromKey(ITEM_DEFS[swing.item]?.key ?? '');
        if (vid === 3779) { offX = 6; offY = p.h * 0.5 + 6; rot = 0; }      // :50343（itemRotation=0）
        else if (vid === 4262) { offX = -6; offY = p.h * 0.5 - 6; rot = 0; } // :50349
        else if (vid >= 0 && STAFF_ITEMS.has(vid)) {
          // :50382-50383 itemLocation = MountedCenter + itemRotation 向量×6（3476 为 14）；
          // ★绘制角 = itemRotation + 0.785*direction（PlayerDrawLayers.cs:3370——法杖斜持
          // 45°，此前漏叠 → 34 件法杖全少转 45°）
          offX = Math.cos(aim) * 6;
          offY = p.h * 0.5 + Math.sin(aim) * 6;
          rot = aim + 0.785;
        } else {
          // 非法杖（枪/弓/悠悠球等）：X=中心-2*dir、Y=MountedCenter-fh/2（:50387），
          // 绘制角 = itemRotation（PlayerDrawLayers.cs:3444）
          offX = -2;
          offY = p.h * 0.5 - fh * 0.5;
          rot = aim;
          // ★握把 origin=(-num9,H/2)（PlayerDrawLayers.cs:3434-3443 + Main.cs:3305
          // DrawPlayerItemPos）：num9 默认 10、逐 id 修正；dir=-1 时原版取 (W+num9,H/2)，
          // 镜像空间等价 → 精灵左上角钉在握点 +（num9, dy）
          const dp = vid >= 0 ? ITEM_DRAW_POS[vid] ?? ITEM_DRAW_POS_DEF : ITEM_DRAW_POS_DEF;
          drawOX = dp[0]; drawOY = dp[1];
        }
        break;
      }
      case 6: {
        // 饮用旋转（:50158-50182，回归药水 2350/4870）：与 7 同构反向
        const n13 = Math.min((1 - pAnim) * 6, 1);
        rot = (1 - n13) * 2 - 1.4;
        if (n13 >= 0.5) { offX = fw * 0.5 - 9 - rot * 12; offY = 38 + rot * 4; }
        else { offX = fw * 0.5 - 9 - rot * 16; offY = 38 + rot; }
        break;
      }
      case 9: {
        // 举杯（:50127-50157，DefaultToHealingPotion/DefaultToFood gulp）：
        // t=clamp((1-pAnim)/0.7)；rot = -2t+0.7；
        // 持位 = GetFrontHandPosition(Full, num11-π/2) 再缩放 Lerp(1.5,1.2,t)+前移 Lerp(8,2,t)
        const t = Math.min(Math.max((1 - pAnim) / 0.7, 0), 1);
        rot = -2 * t + 0.7;
        const num11 = -(Math.PI * 2) / 5 * 0.5 + (1 - t) * ((Math.PI * 2) / 5) + Math.PI / 10;
        // GetFrontHandPosition(Full, num11-π/2)（:49887-49913，dir=1 展开）
        const hx = 10 * Math.cos(num11) - 4 - 3 * Math.sin(num11);
        const hy = 10 * Math.sin(num11) - 2 + 3 * Math.cos(num11);
        offX = hx + (8 + (2 - 8) * t);
        offY = p.h * 0.5 + hy;
        // ★轴心（PlayerDrawLayers.cs:3263-3275 useStyle 9）：origin = Size*(0.5,0.4)
        // （5009/5042/5645 为 (0.26,0.5)；朝左时原版取 X=1-X——镜像空间恒用 dir=1 值）
        const vid9 = viIdFromKey(ITEM_DEFS[swing.item]?.key ?? '');
        if (vid9 === 5009 || vid9 === 5042 || vid9 === 5645) { drawOX = -fw * 0.26; drawOY = -fh * 0.5; }
        else { drawOX = -fw * 0.5; drawOY = -fh * 0.4; }
        break;
      }
      case 13: {
        // 短剑突刺（:50423-50446）：复合手臂持位 GetFrontHandPosition(stretch, itemRotation-π/2)，
        // stretch 按 1-pAnim 档 Quarter(6)/ThreeQuarters(8)/Full(10)；绘制旋转 = itemRotation
        // （本地空间 = 方向化 aim，见函数头）
        const n24 = 1 - pAnim;
        const mult = n24 <= 0.33 ? 6 : n24 <= 0.66 ? 8 : 10;
        rot = aim;
        offX = mult * Math.cos(rot) - 4 - 3 * Math.sin(rot);
        offY = p.h * 0.5 + mult * Math.sin(rot) - 2 + 3 * Math.cos(rot);
        break;
      }
      case 14: {
        // 头侧持（:50604-50617，夜光 4952）：X=中心+6、Y=顶+32+头饰偏移；身体行不改
        offX = 6;
        offY = 32 + HEADGEAR_OFFSET_Y[2];
        break;
      }
      case 15: {
        // 头侧持（:50618-50628，占卜球 5644）：Y=顶+28+头饰偏移
        offX = 6;
        offY = 28 + HEADGEAR_OFFSET_Y[2];
        break;
      }
      case 16: {
        // 委托静持（:50629-50633，独角兽棒 856 = holdStyle 1 特例：X=中心+4、Y=顶+34）
        offX = 4;
        offY = 34;
        break;
      }
      default: {
        // 7/8/10/11/12 等未入表族回退 useStyle 1 挥砍（三段持位与 case 1 同款同步）
        rot = (pAnim - 0.5) * -3.5 - 0.3;
        const seg = pAnim <= 1 / 3 ? swingTier(0) : pAnim < 2 / 3 ? swingTier(1) : swingTier(2);
        offX = seg.x; offY = seg.y;

    }
    if (hide) return;

	public static Rectangle GetDrawHitbox(int type, Player user)
	{
		if (Main.dedServ)
		{
			return Rectangle.Empty;
		}
		Main.instance.LoadItem(type);
		if (ItemID.Sets.IsFood[type])
		{
			return TextureAssets.Item[type].Frame(1, 3, 0, 1);
		}
		switch (type)
		{
		case 75:
			return TextureAssets.Item[type].Frame(1, 8);
		case 520:
		case 521:
		case 547:
		case 548:
		case 549:
		case 575:
		case 3453:
		case 3454:
		case 3455:
		case 3580:
		case 3581:
		case 4068:
		case 4069:
		case 4070:
			return TextureAssets.Item[type].Frame(1, 4);
		default:
			if (Main.itemAnimations[type] != null)
			{
				return Main.itemAnimations[type].GetFrame(TextureAssets.Item[type].Value);
			}
			return TextureAssets.Item[type].Frame();
		}
	}

## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-18T07:02:41.540Z

**📎 ToolResult**

```
	private void AdjustmentsForWolfMount()
	{
		hideEntirePlayer = true;
		weaponDrawOrder = WeaponDrawOrder.BehindBackArm;
		Vector2 vector = new Vector2(10 + drawPlayer.direction * 14, 12f);
		Vector2 vector2 = Position + vector;
		Position.X -= drawPlayer.direction * 10;
		bool flag = heldItem.useStyle == 5 || SelectedDrawnProjectile != null;
		bool num = heldItem.useStyle == 2;
		bool flag2 = heldItem.useStyle == 9;
		bool flag3 = drawPlayer.itemAnimation > 0;
		bool flag4 = heldItem.fishingPole != 0;
		bool flag5 = heldItem.useStyle == 14;
		bool flag6 = heldItem.useStyle == 8;
		bool flag7 = heldItem.holdStyle == 1;
		bool flag8 = heldItem.holdStyle == 2;
		bool flag9 = heldItem.holdStyle == 5;
		if (num)
		{
			ItemLocation += new Vector2(drawPlayer.direction * 14, -4f);
		}
		else if (!flag4)
		{
			if (flag2)
			{
				ItemLocation += (flag3 ? new Vector2(drawPlayer.direction * 18, -4f) : new Vector2(drawPlayer.direction * 14, -18f));
			}
			else if (flag9)
			{
				ItemLocation += new Vector2(drawPlayer.direction * 17, -8f);
			}
			else if (flag7 && drawPlayer.itemAnimation == 0)
			{
				ItemLocation += new Vector2(drawPlayer.direction * 14, -6f);
			}
			else if (flag8 && drawPlayer.itemAnimation == 0)
			{
				ItemLocation += new Vector2(drawPlayer.direction * 17, 4f);
			}
			else if (flag6)
			{
				ItemLocation = vector2 + new Vector2(drawPlayer.direction * 12, 2f);
			}
			else if (flag5)
			{
				ItemLocation += new Vector2(drawPlayer.direction * 5, -2f);
			}
			else if (flag)
			{
				ItemLocation += new Vector2(drawPlayer.direction * 4, -4f);
			}
			else
			{
				ItemLocation = vector2;
			}
		}
	}

	private void AdjustmentsForVelociraptorMount()
	{
		hideEntirePlayerExceptHelmetsAndFaceAccessories = true;
		weaponDrawOrder = WeaponDrawOrder.BehindFrontArm;
		Position.X -= drawPlayer.direction * 14;
		bool flag = drawPlayer.itemAnimation > 0;
		if (heldItem.useStyle == 8 && flag)
		{
			weaponDrawOrder = WeaponDrawOrder.OverFrontArm;
		}
		drawPlayer.ApplyItemPositionOffsetFromMount(ref ItemLocation);
	}

	private void AdjustmentsForRatMount()
	{
		hideEntirePlayer = true;
		weaponDrawOrder = WeaponDrawOrder.BehindBackArm;
	}

	private void AdjustmentsForBatMount()
	{
		hideEntirePlayer = true;
		weaponDrawOrder = WeaponDrawOrder.BehindBackArm;
	}

	private void AdjustmentsForPixieMount()
	{
		hideEntirePlayer = true;
		weaponDrawOrder = WeaponDrawOrder.BehindBackArm;
	}

	private void CreateCompositeData()
	{
		frontShoulderOffset = Vector2.Zero;
		backShoulderOffset = Vector2.Zero;
		usesCompositeTorso = drawPlayer.body > 0 && drawPlayer.body < ArmorIDs.Body.Count && ArmorIDs.Body.Sets.UsesNewFramingCode[drawPlayer.body];
		usesCompositeFrontHandAcc = drawPlayer.handon > 0 && drawPlayer.handon < ArmorIDs.HandOn.Count && ArmorIDs.HandOn.Sets.UsesNewFramingCode[drawPlayer.handon];
		usesCompositeBackHandAcc = drawPlayer.handoff > 0 && drawPlayer.handoff < ArmorIDs.HandOff.Count && ArmorIDs.HandOff.Sets.UsesNewFramingCode[drawPlayer.handoff];

```

  private playerFrameRows(p: Player,
    swing: { useStyle?: number } | null, holdStyleRaw: number,
    mnt: import('../entities/Mounts').MountInstance | null, cart: unknown,
    legArmorId: number,
  ): { body: number; legs: number } {
    const useRow = swing ? this.useStyleBodyRow(swing as never, p) : -1;
    const mountActive = !!(mnt || (p.ridingCart && cart));
    const mtype = p.mount.type;
    const noLegOverride = !!MOUNT_SETS.DoesNotOverrideLegFrames?.[mtype];   // 57-60 滚轴鞋
    const noBodyOverride = !!MOUNT_SETS.DoesNotOverrideBodyFrames?.[mtype];
    const latched = p.grappleProjs.find((g) => g.latched) ?? null;
    const wing = p.equipStats.wing;
    // ShouldDrawWingsThatAreAlwaysAnimated（:30271）——22/28/45 翼腿行 0 门
    const wingsAlways = p.vy !== 0 && !mountActive && !latched
      && !(p.inWater && (p.equipStats.flipper || p.buffs.has(BuffType.FlipperPotion)));
    const leg140 = legArmorId === 140;               // :35786 legs!=140 门（主链跳过）
    // ---- 腿链（:35718-35731 坐骑钉行 / :35786-35855 循环族 / :35858-35877 覆写族）----
    let legs: number;
    if (mountActive && !noLegOverride) {
      legs = (mtype === 23 || mtype === 45 || mtype === 48) ? 0 : 6;   // 普通坐骑行6（含矿车）
      Renderer.swimPhase.delete(p);
    } else if (p.swimTime > 0 && !leg140) {
      // 划水循环 +2/t 行 7..19（:35792-35805）——相位见 swimPhase 注释
      let m0 = Renderer.swimPhase.get(p);
      if (m0 === undefined) m0 = p.miscCounter;
      else if (p.miscCounter < m0) m0 -= 300;        // miscCounter wrap 300 → 虚拟轴滚动
      Renderer.swimPhase.set(p, m0);
      const d = p.miscCounter - m0;                  // 连续游泳 tick 数（虚拟单调）
      let row = 5;                                   // 入水时上一行（vy≠0 分支行 5）
      const adv = Math.floor(d / 4);                 // counter=d×2，每 8 一行
      for (let k = 0; k < adv; k++) {                // 逐行钳（while 语义）
        row += 1;
        if (row < 7) row = 19;
        else if (row > 19) row = 7;
      }
      legs = row;
    } else if (p.vy !== 0 || latched) {
      Renderer.swimPhase.delete(p);                  // vy≠0 分支 counter=0（:35807-35810）
      legs = (wing && (wing.slot === 22 || wing.slot === 28 || wing.slot === 45) && wingsAlways) ? 0 : 5;
    } else if (p.vx !== 0 && p.onGround) {
      Renderer.swimPhase.delete(p);                  // 站立/行走分支 counter=0（:35824/35850）
      // 行走循环（:35817-35847）：counter += |vx|·1.3、每 8 进一行、行 7..19 回绕；
      // 滚轴鞋（IsRollerSkates）满速档每 tick +2（:35830-35833 counter=2.0）。
      // animTime = |vx| 逐 tick 累计（走停清零）→ counter = animTime × 速率
      const skates = !!MOUNT_SETS.IsRollerSkates?.[mtype] && mountActive;
      const ratePerTick = skates && Math.abs(p.vx) >= (p.equipStats.accRunSpeed || 3) ? 2 : 1.3;
      legs = 7 + Math.floor((p.animTime * ratePerTick) / 8) % 13;
    } else {
      Renderer.swimPhase.delete(p);
      legs = 0;
    }
    if (!p.onGround && p.carpetTime > 0) legs = 0;   // 飞毯滑翔腿钉 0(:35858-35863;
    // ★门=滑翔中(airborne)非燃料>0——2026-08-16 水体批把 onGround 重置段写了
    // carpetTime=300 回满,落地恒 300 → 旧门把地面走路腿钉死站立帧(用户实报)
    if (p.performingJump === 'Sandstorm') legs = 0;                  // 沙暴跳腿钉 0（:35865-35877）
    // ---- 身体链（:35874-36186，else-if 序 1:1）----
    let body: number;
    if (useRow >= 0) body = useRow;                       // useStyle 行（:35879-36018）
    else if (holdStyleRaw === 1 || holdStyleRaw === 3 || holdStyleRaw === 5) body = 3;   // :36025-36040
    else if (holdStyleRaw === 2) body = 2;                // :36036
    else if (holdStyleRaw === 4 && p.onGround && p.gravDir === 1) body = 0;             // :36042-36049
    else if (holdStyleRaw === 7) body = 11;               // :36042-36045（物品本身不画）
    else if (p.shieldRaised) body = 10;                   // 盾举行10（:36052-36055）
    else if (mountActive && !noBodyOverride) body = p.mount.bodyFrame;  // :36056-36060（矿车=3）
    else if (latched) {
      body = latched.y < p.y ? 2 : latched.y > p.y + p.h ? 4 : 3;     // :36061-36096
      if (p.gravDir === -1 && body !== 3) body = body === 2 ? 4 : 2;
    }
    else if (p.inWater && !p.inputDown && (p.equipStats.canFloat || p.buffs.has(BuffType.FlipperPotion))
      && (!p.ridingMount || p.mount.type === 37)) body = 10;           // 浮水行10（:36098-36100 + :3710-3722——canFloatInWater 仅 4404 甲件∪buff265,flipper 饰品不授予）
    else if (p.swimTime > 0) body = p.swimTime > 20 ? 0 : p.swimTime > 10 ? 5 : 0;     // :36102-36119
    else if (p.vy !== 0) {
      if (p.eocDash > 0) body = 6;
      else if (p.performingJump === 'Sandstorm' || (!p.onGround && p.carpetTime > 0)) body = 6;  // :36123-36134(同上门修)
      else if (wing) {
        if (wing.slot === 22 || wing.slot === 28 || wing.slot === 45) body = 0;
        else body = (p.vy * p.gravDir < 0) || (p.inputJump && p.vy * p.gravDir > 0) ? 6 : 5;   // :36135-36155
      } else body = 5;
    }
    else if (p.vx !== 0 && p.onGround) body = legs;       // 行走身体同步腿（:36184-36186）
    else body = 0;
    // ---- legs==140 神灯诅咒（3770，:36195-36208；主链已跳过，此处覆写）----
    // 行 = vy≠0?1:0；身体行∈HEADGEAR_ZERO_ROWS（OffsetsPlayerHeadgear.Y==0：
    // {7,8,9,14,15,16}，Main.cs:504-525）→ 行 7；翼 22/28 → 行 0
    if (leg140) {
      legs = p.vy !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
      if (HEADGEAR_ZERO_ROWS.has(body)) legs = 7;
      if (wing && (wing.slot === 22 || wing.slot === 28)) legs = 0;
    }
    if (legArmorId === 217 && (p.sitting || p.sleeping)) legs = 5;   // :36202-36206
    return { body, legs };
  }

  /** 身体帧行复算（47/49 锚点行修正用——与下方纸娃娃/legacy 行选择同规则，
   *  独立复算避免改动并行会话在改的帧选择主体） */
  private playerBodyRow(p: Player,
    swing: { useStyle?: number } | null, staticHoldStyle: number,
    mnt: unknown): number {
    const useRow = swing ? this.useStyleBodyRow(swing as never, p) : -1;
    if (useRow >= 0) return useRow;
    if (mnt || p.ridingCart) return 3;
    if (Math.abs(p.vx) > 0.3 && p.onGround) return 6 + Math.floor(p.animTime / 6) % 14;
    if (staticHoldStyle === 2) return 2;
    if (staticHoldStyle === 4 && p.onGround) return 0;
    if (staticHoldStyle) return 3;
    if (!p.onGround) return p.vy < 0 ? 1 : 4;
    return 0;
  }

  /** 全亮翅膀绘制队列（mainGlow 主纹理 + 全部叠画；光照合成后 flush） */
  private wingGlowQueue: Array<{
    p: Player; slot: number; fr: number; anchor: [number, number];
    wx: number; wy: number;
    /** drawinfo.stealth（叠画消费值）与 s' 档（lit 主纹理用，flush 内 34/51 等取 settled） */
    settled: number; clamp: number;
    vortexTint: [number, number, number] | null;
    /** cWings 染料（SM2 字节码执行描述；null = 无染料） */
    dye: DyeApply | null;
  }> = [];
  /** 均一色调 scratch（叠画 tint 精确化：RGB×tint/255 + A×alpha/255） */
  private wingTintScratch: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;

  /** 队列消费（合成后调用）：局部变换 = worldToScreen(wx,wy) + scale(facing·z, z)。
   *  stealth 逐层公式（drawinfo.stealth = settled）：34/40×s、51 luna×s、44×s、
   *  28/45 White 无 s；叠画 23/27/30×s、43×s²、50×s²、38×s、47 A180 iff s==1、
   *  22 火焰逐次平方（:681 累乘）、extra38=Lerp(colorArmorBody,White,0.5) 光采样 */
  private flushWingGlow(cam: Camera, z: number): void {
    if (!this.wingGlowQueue.length) return;
    const atlas = this.atlas;
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    if (!atlas) { this.wingGlowQueue.length = 0; return; }

  private useStyleBodyRow(swing: { t: number; dur: number; useStyle?: number; aim?: number }, p: Player): number {
    const us = swing.useStyle ?? 1;
    const pAnim = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, swing.t / swing.dur));
    switch (us) {
      case 1:
      case 13:
        // :35882-35890 / :35971-35985：三段 行1/2/3（13 与 1 同表）
        return pAnim > 2 / 3 ? 1 : pAnim > 1 / 3 ? 2 : 3;
      case 2:
      case 3:
        return 3; // :35908 / :35948（3 两段都是行3）
      case 4:
        return 2; // :35959 举过头
      case 9:
        return 0; // :35927 饮用
      case 6: {
        // :35931-35947：(1-pAnim)*6 截 1，≥0.5 → 行3 否则行2
        const n = Math.min((1 - pAnim) * 6, 1);
        return n >= 0.5 ? 3 : 2;
      }
      case 5: {
        // :35986-36011：默认行3；itemRotation*dir < -0.75 → 行2、> 0.6 → 行4。
        // itemRotation = Atan2(aimY*dir, aimX*dir)（:46730）——朝左时与裸 aim 相差 ±π，
        // 此前直接 aim*facing 同源错位。gravDir=-1 行2↔4 互换（:35996-36011）
        const irot = Math.atan2(Math.sin(swing.aim ?? 0) * p.facing, Math.cos(swing.aim ?? 0) * p.facing);
        const n21 = irot * p.facing;
        if (p.gravDir === -1) {
          if (n21 < -0.75) return 4;
          if (n21 > 0.6) return 2;
          return 3;
        }
        if (n21 < -0.75) return 2;
        if (n21 > 0.6) return 4;
        return 3;
      }
      case 8:
        return 0;   // :35963-35965 割草机（DefaultToLawnMower）
      case 11:
        // :35912-35925 高尔夫球杆三段：>0.5→行4、>0.15→行3、else 行2
        return pAnim > 0.5 ? 4 : pAnim > 0.15 ? 3 : 2;
      case 12:
        return 3;   // :35967-35969 吉他
      case 14:
      case 15:
      case 10:
      case 16:
        return -1;
      default:
        return pAnim > 2 / 3 ? 1 : pAnim > 1 / 3 ? 2 : 3;
    }
  }

  /** 使用/挥舞动画（与静持同尺寸：原始像素比例，不放大）。
   *  useStyle 1:1 分族（Player.cs:49916 ItemCheck_ApplyUseStyle switch）：
   *   1 挥砍三段（已验证）/ 2 咀嚼 / 3 雨伞 / 4 举过头 / 5 朝鼠标静持（法杖钉中心）

/**

 * 食物族:ItemID.Sets.IsFood(:258,86 项)全部为竖 3 帧条,原版注册
 * DrawAnimationVertical(int.MaxValue,3) = 恒帧 0(顶部一帧);本表以 FOOD
 * 静态条目等价表达(切片帧高 = 总高/3)。
 * 物品源图是竖排多帧条(如坠星 Item_75=22×208),不切片会整条入画。
 * dur=tick/帧;pingPong=往返;static=true=恒帧 0(IsFood 的 int.MaxValue
 * 静态三帧与 NotActuallyAnimating 族)。
 */
export const ITEM_ANIMATION: Record<number, { dur: number; n: number; pingPong?: boolean; static?: boolean }> = {
  3581: { dur: 4, n: 4 },
  3580: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
  75: { dur: 5, n: 8, pingPong: true },    // 坠星 8 帧 PingPong 旋转
  575: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 547: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 520: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
  548: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 521: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 549: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
  3453: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 3454: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 3455: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
  4068: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },   // NotActuallyAnimating(:3701-3709)
  4069: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },
  4070: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },
  5644: { dur: 7, n: 9 },                 // ScryingOrb(专属着色器,垂直循环近似)
  // ---- 食物族(IsFood :258):竖 3 帧条,原版 int.MaxValue 恒帧 0 ----
  ...Object.fromEntries(FOOD_IDS.map((v) => [v, FOOD])),
};


/** 物品动画当前帧(DrawAnimationVertical.GetFrame 语义;tick=游戏 tick 或等价毫秒换算) */
export function itemAnimFrame(vid: number, tick: number): number {
  const a = ITEM_ANIMATION[vid];
  if (!a || a.static) return 0;
  const span = a.pingPong ? a.n * 2 - 2 : a.n;
  const idx = Math.floor(tick / a.dur) % span;
  return a.pingPong && idx >= a.n ? span - idx : idx;
}

/** 把整条 DrawRect 按帧切片(vid 无动画原样返回) */
export function sliceItemAnimFrame(vid: number, ar: DrawRect, tick: number): DrawRect {
  const a = ITEM_ANIMATION[vid];
  if (!a || a.n <= 1) return ar;
  const fh = ar.sh / a.n;
  const f = Math.min(a.n - 1, itemAnimFrame(vid, tick));
  return { img: ar.img, sx: ar.sx, sy: ar.sy + Math.round(fh * f), sw: ar.sw, sh: Math.round(fh) };
}

export function atlasIconForKey(atlas: SpriteAtlas, key: string): DrawRect | null {
  const vid = VANILLA_ITEM_ICON_MAP[key];
  if (vid !== undefined) return atlas.vicon(vid);
  // 阶段 5:vi_<id>_* 全量物品回退原版图标
  if (key.startsWith('vi_')) {
    const id = parseInt(key.slice(3), 10);
    if (Number.isFinite(id)) return atlas.vicon(id);
  }
  // 阶段 5:place_v_* 放置物品回退用图块贴图首帧作图标
  if (key.startsWith('place_v_')) {
    const tk = key.slice('place_'.length);
    const tid = TILE_BY_KEY[tk];
    if (tid !== undefined) {
      const td = TILE_DEFS[tid];
      // vframeAt 而非 vframe:窄条表(如压板 135 的 16×200 竖条)cols=0 会让 vframe 判越界
      if (td?.vanilla) return atlas.vframeAt(td.vanilla.sheet, 0, 0) ?? atlas.vframe(td.vanilla.sheet, 0, 0);
    }
  }
  return null;
}

// 我们的 item key → 原版物品图标 id（TEdit items.json 核实；Maples 缺图标的用这层）
export const VANILLA_ITEM_ICON_MAP: Record<string, number> = {

And static helper:
```ts
  /** IsFood 手持帧 = 竖 3 帧条第 2 行（GetItemDrawFrame :41901-41903 Frame(1,3,0,1)）；
   *  原条缺失/非 3 整除时回退 atlasIcon 已切帧（帧 0） */
  private static foodHeldFrame(
    raw: { img: CanvasImageSource; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number } | null,
    fallback: { img: CanvasImageSource; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number },
  ) {
    if (!raw || raw.sh < 3 || raw.sh % 3 !== 0) return fallback;
    const fh = raw.sh / 3;
    return { img: raw.img, sx: raw.sx, sy: raw.sy + fh, sw: raw.sw, sh: fh };
  }

{ dx: d * 18, dy: -4 } : { dx: d * 14, dy: -18 };\n      if (useStyle === 8) return { dx: 0, dy: 0, resetX: 14 + d * 12, resetY: 12 + 2 };\n      if (useStyle === 14) return { dx: d * 5, dy: -2 };\n      if (useStyle === 5) return { dx: d * 4, dy: -4 };\n      if (holdStyle === 5) return { dx: d * 17, dy: -8 };\n      if (holdStyle === 1 && !animating) return { dx: d * 14, dy: -6 };\n      if (holdStyle === 2 && !animating) return { dx: d * 17, dy: 4 };\n      return { dx: 0, dy: 0, resetX: 14, resetY: 12 };   // else：ItemLocation = vector2\n    }\n    if (mtype === 54) {\n      // ApplyItemPositionOffsetFromMount（:50671-50770）：仅 animating || holdStyle>0\n      if (!animating && holdStyle <= 0) return null;\n      if (isFishingPole) return { dx: -2, dy: 0 };\n      if (useStyle === 2) return animating ? { dx: 7, dy: -4 } : { dx: 3, dy: 2 };\n      if (useStyle === 9) return animating ? { dx: 14, dy: -10 } : { dx: 3, dy: 2 };\n      if (useStyle === 11) return animating ? { dx: 4, dy: 0 } : { dx: 1, dy: 0 };\n      if (useStyle === 8) return { dx: 6, dy: 0 };\n      if (useStyle === 14) return animating ? { dx: -6, dy: 6 } : { dx: -10, dy: 10 };\n      if (holdStyle === 5) return { dx: 6, dy: 0 };\n      if (holdStyle === 1 && !animating) return { dx: 1, dy: 4 };\n      if (holdStyle === 2) return { dx: 6, dy: 0 };\n      return null;\n    }\n    return null;\n  }",
 "new_string": "  /** 变身坐骑手持物锚点（PlayerDrawSet.cs:385-410 AdjustmentsFor{Wolf,Velociraptor,\n   *  Rat,Bat,Pixie}Mount + Player.cs:50638-50794 ApplyItemPositionOffsetFromMount）：\n   *  52 狼：ItemLocation 表（useStyle 2/9/14/8/5、holdStyle 5/1/2；else 与 useStyle 8\n   *  重置到 vector2 = Position+(10+dir·14,12)——玩家宽 20 折局部锚 (14+dir·δ, 12+δ)；\n   *  狼表无悠悠球/风筝/3542 特判——原版即无，三件是 54 迅猛龙专属档）；\n   *  54 迅猛龙：ApplyItemPositionOffsetFromMount 偏移表（animating=挥舞中 itemAnimation\n   *  >0；静持 animating=false；:50671-50793 全分支 1:1——风筝 flag12 :50713、悠悠球\n   *  flag9 按身体行 num :50730-50743、3542 星云烈焰 flag11 :50744-50760、useStyle 5\n   *  flag3 :50761-50764、default 族按 num :50765-50777；num = bodyFrame.Y/56 由调用\n   *  方传 useStyleBodyRow/静持行）；55/56/61 原版不改 ItemLocation（hideEntirePlayer\n   *  但手持照画在原锚——返回 null 原样）。Position.X 的 −dir·10/−14 只平移玩家本体\n   *  绘制（ItemLocation 为世界绝对坐标不受其影响——手持端无需补）。使用中火把 186\n   *  在 54 上走 (-4,0)·(pos−Center) 角旋转（:50722-50728，异形旋转项登记不近似）。 */\n  /** 悠悠球族（ItemID.cs:1166 Yoyo = Factory.CreateBoolSet，21 件） */\n  private static YOYO_ITEMS = new Set([3262, 3278, 3279, 3280, 3281, 3282, 3283, 3284,\n    3285, 3286, 3287, 3288, 3289, 3290, 3291, 3292, 3315, 3316, 3317, 3389, 5294]);\n  /** 风筝族（ItemID.cs:153 IsAKite = Factory.CreateBoolSet(false, …)，25 件） */\n  private static KITE_ITEMS = new Set([4367, 4368, 4369, 4370, 4371, 4379, 4610, 4611,\n    4612, 4613, 4648, 4649, 4650, 4651, 4669, 4670, 4671, 4674, 4675, 4676, 4677,\n    4681, 4683, 4684]);\n  private mountHeldAnchor(\n    mtype: number, useStyle: number | null, holdStyle: number, animating: boolean,\n    isFishingPole = false,\n    held?: { vid?: number; bodyRow?: number },\n  ): { dx: number; dy: number; resetX?: number; resetY?: number } | null {\n    const d = 1;   // dir=1 空间（offX 语义同域：直接 += / 赋值）\n    if (mtype === 52) {\n      // AdjustmentsForWolfMount（:1785-1842）；钓竿 flag4（fishingPole!=0）豁免原锚\n      if (isFishingPole) return null;\n      if (useStyle === 2) return { dx: d * 14, dy: -4 };\n      if (useStyle === 9) return animating ? { dx: d * 18, dy: -4 } : { dx: d * 14, dy: -18 };\n      if (useStyle === 8) return { dx: 0, dy: 0, resetX: 14 + d * 12, resetY: 12 + 2 };\n      if (useStyle === 14) return { dx: d * 5, dy: -2 };\n      if (useStyle === 5) return { dx: d * 4, dy: -4 };   // flag = us5||SelectedDrawnProjectile（悠悠球线挂中同档）\n      if (holdStyle === 5) return { dx: d * 17, dy: -8 };\n      if (holdStyle === 1 && !animating) return { dx: d * 14, dy: -6 };\n      if (holdStyle === 2 && !animating) return { dx: d * 17, dy: 4 };\n      return { dx: 0, dy: 0, resetX: 14, resetY: 12 };   // else：ItemLocation = vector2\n    }\n    if (mtype === 54) {\n      // ApplyItemPositionOffsetFromMount（:50671-50793）：仅 animating || holdStyle>0\n      if (!animating && holdStyle <= 0) return null;\n      const vid = held?.vid ?? -1;\n      const num = held?.bodyRow ?? 0;              // :50646 num = bodyFrame.Y / 56\n      if (isFishingPole) return { dx: -2, dy: 0 };\n      if (useStyle === 2) return animating ? { dx: 7, dy: -4 } : { dx: 3, dy: 2 };\n      if (useStyle === 9) return animating ? { dx: 14, dy: -10 } : { dx: 3, dy: 2 };\n      if (useStyle === 11) return animating ? { dx: 4, dy: 0 } : { dx: 1, dy: 0 };\n      if (useStyle === 8) return { dx: 6, dy: 0 };\n      if (useStyle === 14) return animating ? { dx: -6, dy: 6 } : { dx: -10, dy: 10 };\n      if (vid >= 0 && Renderer.KITE_ITEMS.has(vid)) return { dx: 4, dy: -4 };   // :50713-50715 风筝（不分动画态）\n      if (holdStyle === 5) return { dx: 6, dy: 0 };\n      if (holdStyle === 1 && !animating) return { dx: 1, dy: 4 };\n      if (holdStyle === 2) return { dx: 6, dy: 0 };\n      if (vid >= 0 && Renderer.YOYO_ITEMS.has(vid)) {                           // :50730-50743 悠悠球按身体行\n        if (num === 2) return { dx: 10, dy: -10 };\n        if (num === 3) return { dx: 8, dy: 0 };\n        if (num === 4) return { dx: 2, dy: 2 };\n        return { dx: 0, dy: 0 };\n      }\n      if (vid === 3542) {                                                       // :50744-50760 星云烈焰按身体行\n        if (num === 2) return { dx: -10, dy: 0 };\n        if (num === 3 || num === 4) return { dx: 10, dy: 0 };\n        return { dx: 0, dy: 0 };\n      }\n      if (useStyle === 5) return { dx: 4, dy: 0 };                              // :50761-50764\n      if (num === 1) return { dx: 1, dy: -1 };          // :50765-50777 default 族按身体行\n      if (num === 2) return { dx: 4, dy: 2 };\n      if (num === 3) return { dx: 2, dy: 0 };\n      return null;                                      // 行 0/4+ = Vector2.Zero 等价\n    }\n    return null;\n  }"
}

             // 期望 0\n  res.ice197_noInput = legs3({ floorTileT: 197, inputX: 0 });           // slippy2 冻史莱姆块 期望 0\n  res.ice_input = legs3({ floorTileT: 161, inputX: 1 });                // 有输入 → 行走循环 7..19\n  res.ground_noInput = legs3({ floorTileT: -1, inputX: 0 });            // 普通地面无输入（击退余速）→ 原版走循环\n  res.skates_noInput = legs3({ mnt: { type: 57 }, inputX: 0 });         // 滚轴鞋 57 无输入 期望 0\n  res.skates_input = legs3({ mnt: { type: 57 }, inputX: 1, vx: 4 });    // 滚轴鞋有输入 → 循环\n  eq('#3 冰161无输入', res.ice_noInput, 0);\n  eq('#3 冻197无输入', res.ice197_noInput, 0);\n  eq('#3 冰有输入走循环', res.ice_input >= 7 && res.ice_input <= 19, true);\n  eq('#3 普地无输入走循环', res.ground_noInput >= 7 && res.ground_noInput <= 19, true);\n  eq('#3 滚轴鞋无输入', res.skates_noInput, 0);\n  eq('#3 滚轴鞋有输入走循环', res.skates_input >= 7 && res.skates_input <= 19, true);\n  Object.assign(p, save3);\n\n  // ---------- #2 mountHeldAnchor 表值（Player.cs:50671-50793 原文数字） ----------\n  const M = (mtype, us, hs, anim, fish, held) => R.mountHeldAnchor.call(g.renderer, mtype, us, hs, anim, fish, held);\n  const yoyoRow = (row) => M(54, 5, 0, true, false, { vid: 3389, bodyRow: row });\n  res.yoyo_r2 = yoyoRow(2); res.yoyo_r3 = yoyoRow(3); res.yoyo_r4 = yoyoRow(4);\n  eq('#2 悠悠球行2', yoyoRow(2), { dx: 10, dy: -10 });\n  eq('#2 悠悠球行3', yoyoRow(3), { dx: 8, dy: 0 });\n  eq('#2 悠悠球行4', yoyoRow(4), { dx: 2, dy: 2 });\n  res.kite = M(54, 5, 0, true, false, { vid: 4367, bodyRow: 3 });       // 风筝不分动画态\n  eq('#2 风筝4367', res.kite, { dx: 4, dy: -4 });\n  res.neb_r2 = M(54, 5, 0, true, false, { vid: 3542, bodyRow: 2 });\n  res.neb_r3 = M(54, 5, 0, true, false, { vid: 3542, bodyRow: 3 });\n  eq('#2 星云烈焰行2', res.neb_r2, { dx: -10, dy: 0 });\n  eq('#2 星云烈焰行3', res.neb_r3, { dx: 10, dy: 0 });\n  res.us5_plain = M(54, 5, 0, true, false, { vid: 120, bodyRow: 3 });   // 非悠悠球/3542 的 us5\n  eq('#2 useStyle5', res.us5_plain, { dx: 4, dy: 0 });\n  res.def_r1 = M(54, 1, 0, true, false, { vid: 1, bodyRow: 1 });\n  res.def_r2 = M(54, 1, 0, true, false, { vid: 1, bodyRow: 2 });\n  res.def_r3 = M(54, 1, 0, true, false, { vid: 1, bodyRow: 3 });\n  eq('#2 default行1', res.def_r1, { dx: 1, dy: -1 });\n  eq('#2 default行2', res.def_r2, { dx: 4, dy: 2 });\n  eq('#2 default行3', res.def_r3, { dx: 2, dy: 0 });\n  res.wolf_yoyo = M(52, 5, 0, true, false, { vid: 3389, bodyRow: 3 });  // 狼表无悠悠球特判 → flag(us5) 档\n  eq('#2 狼+悠悠球=us5档', res.wolf_yoyo, { dx: 4, dy: -4 });\n  res.wolf_yoyo_static = M(52, null, 0, false, false, { vid: 3389 });   // 狼静持悠悠球（holdStyle0）无偏移\n  eq('#2 狼静持悠悠球无偏移', res.wolf_yoyo_static, { dx: 0, dy: 0, resetX: 14, resetY: 12 });\n\n  // ---------- #2 端到端：drawUseItem 真路径（迅猛龙+悠悠球）像素差分 ----------\n  const { ITEM_DEFS } = await import('/src/data/items');\n  const yoyoItem = ITEM_DEFS.findIndex((d) => /^vi_3389_/.test(d?.key ?? ''));\n  res.yoyoItemId = yoyoItem;\n  if (yoyoItem >= 0) {\n    const save2 = { ridingMount: p.ridingMount, mount: p.mount, facing: p.facing, gravDir: p.gravDir };\n    p.ridingMount = true; p.mount = { type: 54 }; p.facing = 1; p.gravDir = 1;\n    const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = 360; cv.height = 300;\n    const c2 = cv.getContext('2d');\n    const shots = {};\n    const spyArgs = [];\n    const orig = R.mountHeldAnchor;\n    R.mountHeldAnchor = function (...a) { spyArgs.push(a.slice(0, 6)); return orig.apply(this, a); };\n    try {\n      for (const [tag, aim] of [['r3', 0], ['r2', -1.2]]) {\n        for (let k = 0; k < 12; k++) {   // 等贴图懒加载就绪\n          c2.clearRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);\n          R.drawUseItem.call(g.renderer, c2, p, { t: 30, dur: 60, item: yoyoItem, useStyle: 5, aim }, null);\n          const d = c2.getImageData(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height).data;\n          let n = 0; for (let i = 3; i < d.length; i += 4) if (d[i] > 8) n++;\n          if (n > 40) { shots[tag] = { n }; break; }\n          await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 250));\n        }\n      }\n    } finally { R.mountHeldAnchor = orig; }\n    Object.assign(p, save2);\n    // 质心差分：行2(10,-10) vs 行3(8,0) → Δ≈(+2,-10)（dir=1，旋转差另有摆动）\n    const centroid = () => {\n      const out2 = {};\n      for (const tag of ['r3', 'r2']) {\n        if (!shots[tag]) return null;\n        const d = c2.getImageData(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height).data;\n        let sx = 0, sy = 0, n = 0;\n        for (let y = 0; y < cv.height; y++) for (let x = 0; x < cv.width; x++) {\n          const a = d[(y * cv.width + x) * 4 + 3];\n          if (a > 8) { sx += x; sy += y; n++; }\n        }\n        out2[tag] = { cx: sx / n, cy: sy / n, n };\n      }\n      return out2;\n    };\n    if (shots.r3 && shots.r2) {\n      c2.clearRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);\n      R.drawUseItem.call(g.renderer, c2, p, { t: 30, dur: 60, item: yoyoItem, useStyle: 5, aim: 0 }, null);\n      const cA = centroid();   // 需两次各自成像——上面循环里最后一次是 r2，重画 r3 取像\n      // 简化：直接复用两次单独成像\n      const snap = (aim) => {\n        c2.clearRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);\n        p.ridingMount = true; p.mount = { type: 54 };\n        R.drawUseItem.call(g.renderer, c2, p, { t: 30, dur: 60, item: yoyoItem, useStyle: 5, aim }, null);\n        const d = c2.getImageData(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height).data;\n        let sx = 0, sy = 0, n = 0;\n        for (let y = 0; y < cv.height; y++) for (let x = 0; x < cv.width; x++) {\n          const a = d[(y * cv.width + x) * 4 + 3];\n          if (a > 8) { sx += x; sy += y; n++; }\n        }\n        return { cx: sx / n, cy: sy / n, n };\n      };\n      const a3 = snap(0), a2 = snap(-1.2);\n      Object.assign(p, save2);\n      res.pixelDiff = { r3: a3, r2: a2, dx: +(a2.cx - a3.cx).toFixed(2), dy: +(a2.cy - a3.cy).toFixed(2) };\n      eq('#2 像素差分dy≈-10', Math.abs(res.pixelDiff.dy + 10) <= 5, true);\n      eq('#2 像素差分dx≈+2', Math.abs(res.pixelDiff.dx - 2) <= 6, true);\n      res.spyWiring = spyArgs.filter(a => a[0] === 54).map(a => ({ us: a[1], anim: a[3], vid: a[5]?.vid, row: a[5]?.bodyRow }));\n      const rowsSeen = new Set(res.spyWiring.map(w => w.row));\n      eq('#2 端到端传参含行2/3', rowsSeen.has(2) && rowsSeen.has(3), true);\n      eq('#2 端到端vid=3389', res.spyWiring.every(w => w.vid === 3389), true);\n    } else { res.fail.push('#2 端到端未成像（贴图未就绪）'); }\n  } else { res.fail.push('#2 找不到 vi_3389_ 悠悠球物品'); }\n\n  // ---------- #4 食物手持帧（GetItemDrawFrame :41901-41903 Frame(1,3,0,1)） ----------\n  const itemOf = (vid) => ITEM_DEFS.findIndex((d) => new RegExp(`^vi_${vid}_`).test(d?.key ?? ''));\n  const bbox = (vid, useStyle) => {\n    const id = itemOf(vid);\n    if (id < 0) return null;\n    const save4 = { ridingMount: p.ridingMount, mount: p.mount, facing: p.facing, gravDir: p.gravDir };\n    p.ridingMount = false; p.mount = save4.mount; p.facing = 1; p.gravDir = 1;\n    const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = 260; cv.height = 260;\n    const c2 = cv.getContext('2d');\n    let r = null;\n    for (let k = 0; k < 12; k++) {\n      c2.clearRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);\n      R.drawUseItem.call(g.renderer, c2, p, { t: 30, dur: 60, item: id, useStyle }, null);\n      const d = c2.getImageData(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height).data;\n      let x0 = 1e9, x1 = -1, y0 = 1e9, y1 = -1, n = 0;\n      for (let y = 0; y < cv.height; y++) for (let x = 0; x < cv.width; x++) {\n        if (d[(y * cv.width + x) * 4 + 3] > 8) { n++; if (x < x0) x0 = x; if (x > x1) x1 = x; if (y < y0) y0 = y; if (y > y1) y1 = y; }\n      }\n      if (n > 4) { r = { w: x1 - x0 + 1, h: y1 - y0 + 1, n }; break; }\n      void d;\n      await new Promise(rr => setTimeout(rr, 250));\n    }\n    Object.assign(p, save4);\n    return r;\n  };\n  res.food969_us2 = bbox(969, 2);    // 12×51 竖3条 → 第2行 12×17\n  res.food353_us9 = bbox(353, 9);    // 18×60 竖3条 → 第2行 18×20\n  res.raw969 = bbox(969, 2);         // （重复成像防懒加载误判，同上）\n  res.mm968_us1 = bbox(968, 1);      // 32×10 单帧整图\n  eq('#4 熟棉花糖969=12×17', res.food969_us2 && res.food969_us2.w === 12 && res.food969_us2.h === 17, true);\n  eq('#4 麦酒353=18×20', res.food353_us9 && res.food353_us9.w === 18 && res.food353_us9.h === 20, true);\n  eq('#4 棉花糖串968=32×10整图', res.mm968_us1 && res.mm968_us1.w === 32 && res.mm968_us1.h === 10, true);\n  res.foodHeldFrame = (() => {   // 静态助手直检：969 原条 12×51 → 行1 12×17\n    const raw = g.renderer.atlas.vicon(969);\n    if (!raw) return null;\n    const f = K.foodHeldFrame(raw, raw);\n    return { rawH: raw.sh, got: [f.sw, f.sh], syShift: f.sy - raw.sy };\n  })();\n  eq('#4 foodHeldFrame(969)行1', res.foodHeldFrame && res.foodHeldFrame.got[0] === 12 && res.foodHeldFrame.got[1] === 17 && res.foodHeldFrame.syShift === 17, true);\n\n  res.pass = res.fail.length === 0;\n  return res;\n}, { timeout: 120000 }).catch(e => ({ crash: String(e), stack: e.stack?.slice(0, 600) }));\n\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(out, null, 1));\nawait browser.close();\n"
}

But spy restore in finally — after awaits inside loop, the game loop may call the spied function concurrently — harmless (just extra pushes).

But WAIT — one real hazard: mutating p (ridingMount=true, mount={type:54}) during awaits → the live game render draws the player as raptor-hidden for a few frames — harmless in headless probe, restored after.

Also the itemOf regex `^vi_969_` — does key for 969 exist? vanilla.json key='CookedMarshmallow' — but ITEM_DEFS keys are our own (vi_969_cooked_marshmallow style). FindIndex may fail if key naming differs. Make itemOf more robust: also match ITEM_DEFS entries via VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID? Simpler: check key contains `vi_969_`. If findIndex fails, res.fail notes. Let me check what vi_ keys look like: from VANILLA_ITEM_ICON_MAP: 'vi_2350_recall_potion', 'vi_188_healing_potion' — pattern `vi_<id>_<slug>` ✔. 969 → 'vi_969_cooked_marshmallow' presumably. OK.

One more: `bbox()` sets `p.mount = save4.mount` but save4 taken inside function each call after previous mutation — sequence: save4 saved BEFORE p.ridingMount=false — I set `p.mount = save4.mount` (unchanged) — but first call happens after #2 section already restored p via Object.assign(p, save2) ✔.

Fix the pixelDiff section. Rewrite file section.

y < cv.height; y++) for (let x = 0; x < cv.width; x++) {\n          const a = d[(y * cv.width + x) * 4 + 3];\n          if (a > 8) { sx += x; sy += y; n++; }\n        }\n        if (n > 40) return { cx: sx / n, cy: sy / n, n };\n        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 250));\n      }\n      return null;\n    };\n    let a3 = null, a2 = null;\n    try { a3 = await snap(0); a2 = await snap(-1.2); }\n    finally { R.mountHeldAnchor = orig; }\n    Object.assign(p, save2);\n    if (a3 && a2) {\n      res.pixelDiff = { r3: a3, r2: a2, dx: +(a2.cx - a3.cx).toFixed(2), dy: +(a2.cy - a3.cy).toFixed(2) };\n      eq('#2 像素差分dy≈-10', Math.abs(res.pixelDiff.dy + 10) <= 5, true);\n      eq('#2 像素差分dx≈+2', Math.abs(res.pixelDiff.dx - 2) <= 6, true);\n      res.spyWiring = spyArgs.filter(a => a[0] === 54).map(a => ({ us: a[1], anim: a[3], vid: a[5]?.vid, row: a[5]?.bodyRow }));\n      const rowsSeen = new Set(res.spyWiring.map(w => w.row));\n      eq('#2 端到端传参含行2/3', rowsSeen.has(2) && rowsSeen.has(3), true);\n      eq('#2 端到端vid=3389', res.spyWiring.every(w => w.vid === 3389), true);\n    } else { res.fail.push('#2 端到端未成像（贴图未就绪）'); }\n  } else { res.fail.push('#2 找不到 vi_3389_ 悠悠球物品'); }"

## 1. 迅猛龙脸饰品通道 → 登记引擎级（未硬造）
原版真身不在 Player.cs:50638（那是 ApplyItemPositionOffsetFromMount），而在 **PlayerDrawLayers.cs**：`DrawPlayer_22_FaceAcc`(:2801-2852) + `DrawPlayer_GetMountOffsetForFaceAcc`(:2773-2799)，坐骑偏移表为 **52 狼 (28,−2)·Directions**、54/55/56/61 走 `ApplyHeadOffsetFromMount − GetHelmetOffsetAddonFromMount`；门 = `ArmorIDs.Face.Sets.CanDrawOnVelociraptorMount`(:2129/:2823/:2843)，本体隐藏语义 `hideEntirePlayerExceptHelmetsAndFaceAccessories`（PlayerDrawSet.cs:1843-1854）。全仓核查：Player/Appearance/PaperDoll 均无 face/faceMask/faceFlower 装备槽、无 AccFace 贴图族 → 按任务约定登记为引擎级缺口并写明补系统时的偏移表，注释落在 Renderer.ts:7765-7774。

## 2. 深层偏移档 → 补齐（且纠正归属：三档是迅猛龙 54 表，不是狼 52）
- 原版 `ApplyItemPositionOffsetFromMount`(Player.cs:50638-50794) **只处理坐骑 54（Velociraptor，MountID.cs:141）**；狼 52 的表在另一处 PlayerDrawSet.cs:1785-1842 `AdjustmentsForWolfMount`，逐档比对**本已 1:1 无缺**（狼表原版就没有悠悠球/风筝/3542）。3542 = **Nebula Blaze 星云烈焰**（vanilla.json 核实，非天空龙杖）。
- Renderer.ts:8324-8382 补齐 54 表五档（含此前也缺的 useStyle 5 与 default 按身体行档）：风筝 IsAKite 25 件 (4,−4)（:50713）；悠悠球 Yoyo 21 件按身体行 行2 (10,−10)/行3 (8,0)/行4 (2,2)（:50730-50743）；3542 行2 (−10,0)/行3·行4 (10,0)（:50744-50760）；useStyle 5 (4,0)（:50761）；default 行1 (1,−1)/行2 (4,2)/行3 (2,0)（:50765-50777）。调用端两处传 `{vid, bodyRow}`：挥舞期 = `useStyleBodyRow`（useStyle 5 的 2/3/4 行恰为悠悠球/3542 分档），静持期 = hold 行（hold2→2、hold≥1→3）。

## 3. 冰面滑行无输入腿行 0
原版条件精确为 `(slippy || slippy2 || windPushed || 滚轴鞋) && !controlLeft && !controlRight`（Player.cs:35818-35826）——**不是 wet/onIce**。Renderer.ts:6678-6691：`floorTileT∈{197}∪{161,162,127,163,164,200,659}∪IsRollerSkates` 且 `inputX===0` → 腿钉行 0；windPushed 本引擎无风推模拟（:11432 未移植）注明不列——普通地面无输入滑行（击退余速）原版就走行走循环，行为不冲突。

## 4. 棉花糖手持帧收口
原版 `GetItemDrawFrame`(Player.cs:41896-41916) ≡ `GetDrawHitbox`(Item.cs:49192-49216)：**IsFood 族手持取竖 3 帧条第 2 行 `Frame(1,3,0,1)`**（掉落物动画恒顶行是另一套取帧）；**968 棉花糖串非 IsFood、无动画 → 32×10 整图直画**（vanilla.json 实测 968=32×10、969=12×51、353=18×60）。实现：`foodHeldFrame` 助手（Renderer.ts:8385-8394）+ drawUseItem/静持两路接入（:8404-8410/:7548-7552）；同链顺带补 968 锚 X=+8（:49606）、856 (4,34)、353 (6,中心+11)、IsFood (4,中心+fh/2)（:49709-49713）。
